# Refuting Synergistic Arguments on Salvation (Provisionism, Universalism, Works & Losing Salvation)

**[00:00:00]** Synergism comes in many forms and many flavors, but it's all wrong.

**[00:00:08]** Today we're going to refute synergistic arguments on salvation.
